sudhīsampanna - śrīhemādrināmaka mahāpaṇḍitena viracitoyaṃ caturvvarga-
cintāmaṇināmā smṛtinibandha: vrata-dāna- tīrtha pariśeṣakhaṇḍairvibhaktī-
kṛtaśca | tatra vratakhaṇḍaṃ dānakhaṇḍaṃca sosāiṭī samājādhyakṣa
mahodayānāmnamatyā paṇḍitāgraṇī svargīyabharatacandraśiromaṇi mahodayānāṃ
pariśodhanaprayatnena mudritamabhūt | tadanantaramukta mahodayānāṃ
paralokāvāptau pariśeṣakhaṇḍāntargataśrāddhakhaṇḍa mattā samājādhyakṣa
mahodayānumatyā āvayoḥ pariśodhanaprayatnena ca mudritaṃ | pariśodhane trīṇi
ādarśapustakāni āvayo sahāyabhūtāni āsan| teṣāṃ dvaya kakhacihnitaṃ
vārāṇasyā labdhaṃ eka gacihanita kalikātā-saṃskṛtavidyāmandira-
pustakālayāllabdha| etasmin pustakatraye dvaya pariśuddha, ekamapariśuddha,
yatra pustakatraye saṃśayo jāta tatra smārttaśalapāṇi-manvādismṛti-
nivandhānā sāhāyyena pariśodhita | anantaraṃ pariśeṣakhaṇḍāntargata-
kālakhaṇḍasya mudraṇaṃ bhaviṣyati | sambhāvyate yadetanmudraṇa
mahātmanāmupakārāya bhaviṣyati |

māyāstrimuhattau'tha sarvvakavaciṣkṛta iti ||
śrataeva tatkālavihita karma mātropayogitāmasya vibhāgasyāha parāśaraḥ |
lekhāprabhṛtyathādityānmuhūrttastraya eva ca |
prātastu ma smṛtaḥ kālo bhāgaścāhaḥ ma pañcamaḥ ||
maṅgavatrimuharteau'tha madhyāhnastatmamastataḥ |
tatastrayo mutaśca tvaparāhī viśiṣyate ||
pañcamo'pi dināṃśa ya ma māyāha iti smṛtaḥ |

udayamānamya bhāsvanmaṇḍalamya rekhāvatprakāśamāna: prathamopalambha-
yogyabhāgo 'lekhā' 'śrādityāt' śrādityodayāta| tathāca lekhā-

ukraṃ hi skandapurāṇe |

śayanāmanāni tathā bhūmayo vāhanāni ca |
śrāddheṣvetāni yo dadyādaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
sarpiḥpūrṇāni pātrāṇi śrāddhe mallatya dāpayet |
kumbhādidodhenūnāṃ bīnāṃ phalamaśrute |
yatheṣṭāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet ||
'puṇḍarīkaḥ ' kratuviśeṣaḥ |
ramyamāvamathaṃ dattvā rājasvayaphalaṃ labheta |
kṛpārāma - taḍāgāni kṣetra ghoṣagṭahāṇi ca |
gāṃ dattvā modate svarge nityamācandratārakaṃ ||
svāstīrṇa prayanaṃ dattvā śrāddhe ravivaṣitaṃ |
pitarastasya tuṣyanti svarge cānantyamaśrute ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
aajraupyapācāṇi manojñāni śubhāni ca |

[10 a0 |
ataḥ sarvvaśākhā kalpasvatragṭahyā tipurāṇetihāmā cārā vagatān
padārthānupasaṃhṛtya prayogo'nuṣṭheya iti siddham |
acārthe liṅgadarśanam brahmapurāṇe |
gameṇa bhojitā vimā munyukrena yathāvidhi |

purāṇoko vidhiścaiva vaiśvada vikapūrvakaḥ ||
'munna' munibhiḥ preritana | 'vaidikā' vedavicitapiṇḍapita-

striyaḥ paviṣamatulaṃ netā duṣyanti karhicit|
māmi māmi rajo lāmāṃ duṣkṛtānyapakarṣati ||
pūrva striyaḥ surairbhukā: sāma- gandharva-ṛibhiḥ |
taiśva dattavaga yetā naiva duṣyanti karhicit ||
tāmāṃ māmā dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikhita giraṃ |
agniśca sarvabhakṣyasvaṃ tasmānniṣkamamāḥ striyaḥ ||
cauṇi striyaḥ pātakāṇi loke dharmavido viduḥ |
bharbaṃdho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanaṃ |
balākāpā vā corahamāgatāpivā |

596
caturvarga cintāmayau pariśeṣakha rahe
[10 ba0 |
śudhyeta rajasā mārī nadī vegavatī yathā ||
svayaṃ vipratipannā vā athavā vipramāditā |
śratyantadūṣitā nārī nāsyāstyāgo vidhīyate ||
striyaḥ pavitramatulaṃ netā daṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajeo yārsā duṣkṛtānyapakarṣati ||
pūrva striyaḥ surairbhukā : soma - gandharva - vahnibhiḥ |
momastāsāṃ dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giraṃ |
pāvakaḥ sarvamedhyatvaṃ tasmānniṣkamamāḥ striyaḥ ||
mājñavarukyaḥ |
māmaḥ śaucaṃ dadau strīṇāṃ gandharva śubhāṃ giraṃ |
pāvakaḥ sarvamedhyatvaṃ medhyā vai yoṣito hyataḥ ||
vyabhicārādṛtau śadbhirgarbhe tyāgo vidhīyate |
bharṭa garbhabadhādau ca tathā mahati pātake ||
khyadhikāre paiṭhīnasiḥ |
cauṇi pātakāni bhartṛbadho brahmabadhaḥ svagarbhapātanaceti | khatau
māmi māmi śudhyantītyāha |
yamaḥ |
māsi māmi rajastamāṃ duṣkṛtānyapakarṣati |
puṣpakālamupāsitvā ṛtukālena śudhyati ||
seobhantāmāṃ dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣita giraṃ |
śraca sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tasmāniṣkamamāḥ striyaḥ ||
balātkāropakā vā corahastagatā ca thā |
